Lemma 4.17
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with a finite
affine open covering
X=\bigcup_{\alpha}U_{\alpha}
.
Denote by
j_{\alpha}\colon U_{\alpha}\longrightarrow X
the open immersion morphisms.
Let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
Then any quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-module
\mathcal{M}
on
X
is a submodule of
a finite direct sum
\bigoplus_{\alpha}j_{\alpha}{}_{*}\mathcal{C}_{\alpha}
for
some
\mathcal{A}|_{U_{\alpha}}
-cotorsion quasi-coherent

\mathcal{A}|_{U_{\alpha}}
-modules
\mathcal{C}_{\alpha}
on
U_{\alpha}
.

Proof.

Similar to
Lemmas
4.4

and
4.11
.
Notice that all injective
\mathcal{A}(U_{\alpha})
-modules are cotorsion
by the definition.
∎

Theorem 4.18
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with a finite
affine open covering
X=\bigcup_{\alpha}U_{\alpha}
.
Let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
and
\mathcal{M}
be
a quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-module.
In this context:

(a)
there exists a short exact sequence of quasi-coherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules
0\longrightarrow\mathcal{C}^{\prime}\longrightarrow\mathcal{F}\longrightarrow\mathcal{M}\longrightarrow 0
on
X

such that
\mathcal{F}
is a flat quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-module and
\mathcal{C}^{\prime}
is
an
\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-module;

(b)
there exists a short exact sequence of quasi-coherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules
0\longrightarrow\mathcal{M}\longrightarrow\mathcal{C}\longrightarrow\mathcal{F}^{\prime}\longrightarrow 0
on
X

such that
\mathcal{C}
is an
\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-module
and
\mathcal{F}^{\prime}
is a flat quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-module;

(c)
a quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-module on
X
is
\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion
if and only if it is a direct summand of a finitely iterated extension
of the direct images of
\mathcal{A}|_{U_{\alpha}}
-cotorsion quasi-coherent

\mathcal{A}|_{U_{\alpha}}
-modules in the abelian category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
.

Proof.

Similar to
Theorems
4.5

and
4.12
.
Part (a) follows immediately from
Lemma
4.15
, since the class
of
\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules is preserved by
extensions and direct images from affine open subschemes.
Part (b) follows from part (a) and
Lemma
4.17
by virtue of
the Salce lemma.
Concerning part (c), one has
\Ext^{1}_{\mathcal{A}}(\mathcal{F}^{\prime},\mathcal{D})=0
for all
flat quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
\mathcal{F}^{\prime}
and all
\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion
quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
\mathcal{D}
on
X
by the definition.
∎

Corollary 4.19
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme, and let
\mathcal{A}
be
a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
Then a quasi-coherent left
\mathcal{A}
-module
\mathcal{F}
is flat if and only if

\Ext^{1}_{\mathcal{A}}(\mathcal{F},\mathcal{C})=0
for all
\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion quasi-coherent left

\mathcal{A}
-modules
\mathcal{C}
.

Proof.

Similar to
Corollaries
4.6
(a)
and
4.13
(a).
∎

Remark 4.20
.

Similarly to Remarks
4.7

and
4.14
above, the assertions
of Theorem
4.18
(a–b)
and Corollaries
4.16

and
4.19
can be summarized by
saying that the pair of classes (flat quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules,

\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules) is a hereditary complete
cotorsion pair in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
.
On the other hand,
Theorem
4.18
(c) is
an extra piece of information which one obtains from our proof of
Theorem
4.18
(a–b).

The assertion of
Theorem
4.18
(c)
can be expressed by saying that the class of
\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion
quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules on a quasi-compact semi-separated
scheme
X
is
antilocal
in the sense of
[
54
, Section 4]
.
This is the main result of this
Section
4.3
for our purposes
in this paper.

4.4.
Antilocal locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules

Let
X
be a scheme with an open covering
\mathbf{W}
.
A
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent cosheaf
\mathfrak{P}
on
X
is said to be

antilocal

[
46
, Section 4.3]
if the functor

\Hom^{X}(\mathfrak{P},{-})
takes short exact sequences of locally injective

\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent cosheaves on
X
to short exact
sequences of abelian groups.
In particular, any contraherent cosheaf on an affine scheme
U

with the open covering
\{U\}
is antilocal.

We refer to
[
46
, Section 4.3]
for substantial results about
antilocal
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent cosheaves on quasi-compact
semi-separated schemes
X
.
In particular, the class of all such cosheaves does not depend on
the open covering
\mathbf{W}
, and all of them are (globally) contraherent
on
X

[
46
, Corollaries 4.3.5(c) and 4.3.7]
.
The class of all antilocal contraherent cosheaves on
X
is closed
under extensions, kernels of admissible epimorphisms, direct summands,
and direct products in
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}

[
46
, Corollaries 4.3.3(b)
and 4.3.10]
.
The class of antilocal contraherent cosheaves is preserved by
the direct images with respect to all morphisms of quasi-compact
semi-separated schemes
[
46
, Corollary 4.6.3(a)]
.

Let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
We will say that a
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module
\mathfrak{P}

on
X
(in the sense of the definition in
Section
3.3
)
is
X
-antilocal
if the underlying
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
cosheaf (of
\mathcal{O}_{X}
-modules) of
\mathfrak{P}
is antilocal.

We will also say that a
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module
\mathfrak{P}

is
\mathcal{A}
-antilocal
if the functor
\Hom^{\mathcal{A}}(\mathfrak{P},{-})
takes short
exact sequences of
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules on
X
(in the sense of
Section
3.4
) to short exact
sequences of abelian groups.
In particular, any contraherent
\mathcal{B}
-module on an affine scheme
U

with the open covering
\{U\}
and a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra
\mathcal{B}

over
U
is antilocal, because all short exact sequences of

\mathcal{B}
-locally injective contraherent
\mathcal{B}
-modules on
U
are split
(the category
\mathcal{B}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{\mathcal{B}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}
being equivalent to the category

\mathcal{B}(U){\operatorname{\mathsf{--Mod}}}^{\mathsf{inj}}
of injective modules over the ring
\mathcal{B}(U)
;
see Section
3.4
).

Lemma 4.21
.

For any scheme
X
with an open covering
\mathbf{W}
and any quasi-coherent
quasi-algebra
\mathcal{A}
over
X
, all
\mathcal{A}
-antilocal
\mathbf{W}
-locally
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules on
X
are
X
-antilocal.

Proof.

The argument uses the functor
\Cohom_{X}
from quasi-coherent
quasi-modules to
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent cosheaves on
X
, which
was defined in Section
3.2
.
For any locally injective
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent cosheaf
\mathfrak{J}

on
X
, we have an
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-module
\Cohom_{X}(\mathcal{A},\mathfrak{J})
on
X
(by
Lemmas
3.7
(d)
and
1.6
(d),
and the discussion in Section
3.3
).
For any
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent cosheaf
\mathfrak{P}
on
X
,
the adjunction isomorphism of abelian groups
\Hom^{X}(\mathfrak{P},\mathfrak{J})\simeq\Hom^{\mathcal{A}}(\mathfrak{P},\Cohom_{X}(\mathcal{A},\mathfrak{J}))
holds by
formula (
14
).

Now if
0\longrightarrow\mathfrak{J}^{\prime}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{J}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{J}^{\prime\prime}\longrightarrow 0
is a short exact
sequence in the exact category
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathsf{lin}}
, then
0\longrightarrow\Cohom_{X}(\mathcal{A},\mathfrak{J}^{\prime})\longrightarrow\Cohom_{X}(\mathcal{A},\mathfrak{J})\longrightarrow\Cohom_{X}(\mathcal{A},\mathfrak{J}^{\prime\prime})\longrightarrow 0
is a short exact sequence in the exact category

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}
.
The assertion of the lemma follows from these observations.
∎

Let
X
be a scheme with an open covering
\mathbf{W}
and
\mathcal{A}
be
a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
Let us denote temporarily by
\Ext^{X,*}({-},{-})
and

\Ext^{\mathcal{A},*}({-},{-})
the Ext groups computed in the exact
categories
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
and
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
, respectively
(see
[
46
, Section 4.3]
and
Remark
4.26
below for a discussion).

The following lemma and corollary are dual-analogous to
Lemma
4.1
and
Corollary
4.2
(as well as
to Lemma
4.8
and
Corollary
4.9
).

Lemma 4.22
.

Let
X
be a semi-separated scheme with an open covering
\mathbf{W}
,
let
U\subset X
be an affine open subscheme subordinate to
\mathbf{W}

with the open immersion morphism
j\colon U\longrightarrow X
, and let
\mathcal{A}
be
a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
Let
\mathfrak{P}
be a
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module on
X
such
that its underlying
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent cosheaf (of

\mathcal{O}_{X}
-modules) has the property that
\Ext^{X,1}(\mathfrak{P},\mathfrak{K})=0

for all locally injective
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent cosheaves

\mathfrak{K}
on
X
.
Let
\mathfrak{I}
be an
\mathcal{A}|_{U}
-locally injective contraherent

\mathcal{A}|_{U}
-module on
U
.
Then one has

(a)

\Ext^{\mathcal{A},1}(\mathfrak{P},j_{!}\mathfrak{I})=0
;

(b)
assuming that
X
is also quasi-compact,

\Ext^{\mathcal{A},n}(\mathfrak{P},j_{!}\mathfrak{I})=0
for all
n\geq 1
.

Proof.

Part (a): the equivalence of categories
\mathcal{A}|_{U}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}\simeq\mathcal{A}(U){\operatorname{\mathsf{--Mod}}}^{\mathcal{O}(U){\operatorname{\mathsf{-cta}}}}
identifies
\mathcal{A}|_{U}
-locally injective
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules on
U
with injective
left
\mathcal{A}(U)
-modules.
For any associative ring homomorphism
R\longrightarrow A
, all injective
left
A
-modules are direct summands of
A
-modules of the form

\Hom_{R}(A,J)
, where
J
ranges over injective left
R
-modules.
In particular, all injective left
\mathcal{A}(U)
-module are direct summands
of
\mathcal{A}(U)
-modules of the form
\Hom_{\mathcal{O}(U)}(\mathcal{A}(U),J)
, where

J
ranges over injective
\mathcal{O}_{X}(U)
-modules.
Therefore, it suffices to consider the case when
\mathfrak{I}[U]=\Hom_{\mathcal{O}(U)}(\mathcal{A}(U),J)
.

Denoting by
\mathfrak{J}
the locally injective contraherent cosheaf on
U

corresponding to the
\mathcal{O}(U)
-module
J
, we have

\mathfrak{I}=\Cohom_{U}(\mathcal{A}|_{U},\mathfrak{J})
.
By the projection
formula (
26
),
we have
j_{!}\mathfrak{I}=j_{!}\Cohom_{U}(j^{*}\mathcal{A},\mathfrak{J})\simeq\Cohom_{X}(\mathcal{A},j_{!}\mathfrak{J})
.
Notice that
\mathfrak{K}=j_{!}\mathfrak{J}
is a locally injective contraherent cosheaf
on
X
(as
j
is a flat affine morphism; see
Section
2.4
).

Now we argue similarly to
[
54
, proof of Lemma 1.7(c)]
.
Suppose given a short exact sequence
0\longrightarrow\Cohom_{X}(\mathcal{A},\mathfrak{K})\longrightarrow\mathfrak{Q}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{P}\longrightarrow 0
in the exact category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.
Consider its underlying short exact sequence in
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.
The unit morphism
\mathcal{O}_{X}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}
of the quasi-coherent
quasi-algebra
\mathcal{A}
induces a natural (adjunction) morphism

\Cohom_{X}(\mathcal{A},\mathfrak{K})\longrightarrow\mathfrak{K}
in
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.
Consider the pushout diagram

\textstyle{0\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\Cohom_{X}(\mathcal{A},\mathfrak{K})\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\mathfrak{Q}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\mathfrak{P}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{0}
\textstyle{0\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\mathfrak{K}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\mathfrak{R}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{\mathfrak{P}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{0}

in the exact category
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}
.
By assumption, we have
\Ext^{X,1}(\mathfrak{P},\mathfrak{K})=0
, so the short
exact sequence
0\longrightarrow\mathfrak{K}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{R}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{P}\longrightarrow 0
in

X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
splits.
Let
\mathfrak{R}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{K}
be the splitting morphism.
The composition
\mathfrak{Q}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{R}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{K}
provides the dotted diagonal
arrow on the diagram, making both the triangles on the left-hand side
of the diagram commutative.
By the adjunction formula (
14
), the morphism

\mathfrak{Q}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{K}
in
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
corresponds to a morphism

\mathfrak{Q}\longrightarrow\Cohom_{X}(\mathcal{A},\mathfrak{K})
in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.
The latter morphism splits the original short exact sequence

0\longrightarrow\Cohom_{X}(\mathcal{A},\mathfrak{K})\longrightarrow\mathfrak{Q}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{P}\longrightarrow 0
in the exact
category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
, as desired.

Part (b): for a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme
X
, the condition
on the
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module
\mathfrak{P}
in the formulation
of the lemma just means that
\mathfrak{P}
is

X
-antilocal
[
46
, Corollary 4.3.3(a)]
.

Choose a finite affine open covering
X=\bigcup_{\alpha}U_{\alpha}
of
the scheme
X
subordinate to
\mathbf{W}
, and denote by
j_{\alpha}\colon U_{\alpha}\longrightarrow X
the open immersion morphisms.
For any
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module
\mathfrak{Q}
on
X
,
the natural morphism
\bigoplus_{\alpha}j_{\alpha}{}_{!}j_{\alpha}^{!}\mathfrak{Q}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{Q}
(given by
the adjunction (
17
))
is an admissible epimorphism in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
, as one can see from
the Čech resolution (
23
).
The
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent (in fact, contraherent)
\mathcal{A}
-module

\bigoplus_{\alpha}j_{\alpha}{}_{!}j_{\alpha}^{!}\mathfrak{Q}
on
X
is
X
-antilocal
according to the discussion in
[
46
, Section 4.3]
.

The class of all
X
-antilocal (
\mathbf{W}
-locally) contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules is closed under kernels of admissible epimorphisms
in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
, since the class of all antilocal contraherent
cosheaves on
X
is closed under kernels of admissible epimorphisms
in
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
by
[
46
, Corollary 4.3.3(b)]
(see
also
[
46
, Corollaries 4.3.5(c) and 4.3.7]
).
For all the reasons mentioned above, part (b) follows from part (a)
by
[
46
, Lemma 7.1]
.
∎

Corollary 4.23
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
and
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
Let
\mathfrak{P}
be a
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module on
X
.
Then the following conditions are equivalent:

(1)

\mathfrak{P}
is
X
-antilocal;

(2)

\mathfrak{P}
is
\mathcal{A}
-antilocal;

(3)

\Ext^{\mathcal{A},1}(\mathfrak{P},\mathfrak{J})=0
for all
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective

\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
\mathfrak{J}
on
X
;

(4)

\Ext^{\mathcal{A},n}(\mathfrak{P},\mathfrak{J})=0
for all
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective

\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
\mathfrak{J}
on
X
and
all
n\geq 1
.

Proof.

The implications (4)
\Longrightarrow
(3)
\Longrightarrow
(2)
are obvious (and hold for any scheme
X
).
The implication (2)
\Longrightarrow
(1) holds by
Lemma
4.21
(for any scheme
X
).

It remains to prove (1)
\Longrightarrow
(4).
The argument is based on the Čech resolution and
Lemma
4.22
(b).
Once again, for a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme
X
,
condition (1) implies the condition on
\mathfrak{P}
in the formulation of
Lemma
4.22
,
by
[
46
, Corollary 4.3.3(a)]
.
Let
X=\bigcup_{\alpha=1}^{N}U_{\alpha}
be a finite affine open
covering of
X
subordinate to
\mathbf{W}
.
Then the Čech
resolution (
23
)

0\mskip 1.5mu\relbar\joinrel\relbar\joinrel\rightarrow\mskip 1.5muj_{1,\dotsc,N}{}_{!}j_{1,\dotsc,N}^{!}\mathfrak{J}\mskip 1.5mu\relbar\joinrel\relbar\joinrel\rightarrow\mskip 1.5mu\dotsb\\
\mskip 1.5mu\relbar\joinrel\relbar\joinrel\rightarrow\mskip 1.5mu\bigoplus\nolimits_{1\leq\alpha<\beta\leq N}j_{\alpha,\beta}{}_{!}j_{\alpha,\beta}^{!}\mathfrak{J}\mskip 1.5mu\relbar\joinrel\relbar\joinrel\rightarrow\mskip 1.5mu\bigoplus\nolimits_{\alpha=1}^{N}j_{\alpha}{}_{!}j_{\alpha}^{!}\mathfrak{J}\mskip 1.5mu\relbar\joinrel\relbar\joinrel\rightarrow\mskip 1.5mu\mathfrak{J}\mskip 1.5mu\relbar\joinrel\relbar\joinrel\rightarrow\mskip 1.5mu0

is a finite resolution of
\mathfrak{J}
by the direct images of

\mathcal{A}|_{U}
-locally injective contraherent
\mathcal{A}|_{U}
-modules from
affine open subschemes
U=U_{\alpha_{1}}\cap\dotsb\cap U_{\alpha_{k}}\subset X
in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.
By Lemma
4.22
(b),
we have
\Ext^{\mathcal{A},n}(\mathfrak{P},j_{!}j^{!}\mathfrak{J})=0
for all
n\geq 1
, where

j\colon U\longrightarrow X
is the open immersion morphism.
The desired Ext vanishing (4) follows.
∎

4.5.
Antilocality of antilocal contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open covering

\mathbf{W}
and
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.

By Corollary
4.23
, the classes
of
\mathcal{A}
-antilocal and
X
-antilocal
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules on
X
coincide.
Moreover, by
[
46
, Corollaries 4.3.5(c) and 4.3.7]
, all

X
-antilocal
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules on
X

are (globally) contraherent, and the class of such
\mathcal{A}
-modules
does not depend on the open covering
\mathbf{W}
.

Accordingly, from now on, in the context of quasi-compact
semi-separated schemes, we will speak simply of
antilocal
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
instead of “
\mathcal{A}
-antilocal

\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules” or “
X
-antilocal

\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules”.

Lemma 4.24
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
and
X=\bigcup_{\alpha}U_{\alpha}
be a finite affine
open covering of
X
subordinate to
\mathbf{W}
.
Let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
and
\mathfrak{M}
be
a
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module on
X
.
Then there exists a short exact sequence of
\mathbf{W}
-locally
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
0\longrightarrow\mathfrak{M}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{J}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{P}\longrightarrow 0

on
X
such that
\mathfrak{J}
is an
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective

\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module and
\mathfrak{P}
is a finitely
iterated extension of the direct imags of contraherent

\mathcal{A}|_{U_{\alpha}}
-modules from
U_{\alpha}
in the exact category

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
(or
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}
).

Proof.

This is a version of
[
54
, Proposition 5.2]

and
[
46
, Lemma 4.3.2 or Proposition B.6.4]
.
The same construction, which is the locally contraherent cosheaf
(dual-analogous) version of the construction of
Lemma
4.3
, is applicable
in the situation at hand.
The local nature of the
\mathcal{A}
-local injectivity property of
locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules (see
Section
3.4
) plays a key role.
It is also important that the class of
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective

\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules is preserved by
the direct images with respect to
(\mathbf{W},\mathbf{T})
-affine open immersions of
schemes (see Sections
3.5
–
3.6
), as well as by extensions
and cokernels of admissible monomorphisms in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.
Of course, one also has to use the fact that every

\mathcal{A}(U_{\alpha})
-module is a submodule of an injective

\mathcal{A}(U_{\alpha})
-module.
∎

Lemma 4.25
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
and
X=\bigcup_{\alpha}U_{\alpha}
be a finite affine
open covering of
X
subordinate to
\mathbf{W}
.
Denote by
j_{\alpha}\colon U_{\alpha}\longrightarrow X
the open immersion morphisms.
Let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
and
\mathfrak{M}
be
a
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module on
X
.
Then there exists an admissible epimorphism onto
\mathfrak{M}
in

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
from a finite direct sum
\bigoplus_{\alpha}j_{\alpha}{}_{!}\mathfrak{M}_{\alpha}
for some contraherent

\mathcal{A}|_{U_{\alpha}}
-modules
\mathfrak{M}_{\alpha}
on
U_{\alpha}
.

Proof.

This was explained in the proof of
Lemma
4.22
(b).
∎

Remark 4.26
.

Now we can present the discussion of the notation

\Ext^{\mathcal{A},*}({-},{-})
that was promised in
Section
4.4

(cf.
[
46
, Section 4.3]
).
Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
and
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
First of all, the Ext groups in the category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
do not
depend on the covering
\mathbf{W}
and agree with the Ext groups in
the whole category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}
of locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
on
X
.

Indeed, the full exact subcategory
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
is closed under
extensions and kernels of admissible epimorphisms in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}

(by the definition of the exact categories of locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules, this assertion holds just because it holds for the exact
categories of locally contraherent cosheaves of
\mathcal{O}_{X}
-modules;
see Sections
2.3

and
3.3
).
Furthermore, every object of
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}
is the target of an admissible
epimorphism from an object of
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
(and even
from an antilocal contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module on
X
)
by Lemma
4.25
.
So the dual version of
[
36
, Theorem 12.1(b)]
or the dual
version of
[
35
, Proposition 13.2.2(i)]
is applicable.
See also
[
46
, Proposition A.2.1 or A.3.1(a)]

(or Proposition
5.1
below) for further details.

For the same reasons (up to inverting the arrows), the Ext groups
computed in the exact categories of
X
-locally cotorsion,

\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion, and
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective
\mathbf{W}
-locally
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
,
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
, and
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}
(and also in

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
,
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
, and

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}
).
Indeed, the full exact subcategories
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
,
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
, and
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}
are closed
under extensions and cokernels of admissible monomorphisms in

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
, and we have just shown in
Lemma
4.24
that any
\mathbf{W}
-locally
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module has an admissible monomorphism into
an
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module
in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.
Furthermore, one has
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
(see
Section
3.4
).

When the quasi-coherent quasi-algebra
\mathcal{A}
is flat as a quasi-coherent
sheaf on
X
with respect to its right
\mathcal{O}_{X}
-module structure,
the assertions of the previous paragraph also apply to the exact
categories of
X
-locally injective locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}
and
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}
, because one has

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}
in this case.

The conclusion of this discussion is that the notation

\Ext^{\mathcal{A},*}({-},{-})
for the Ext groups in the exact categories
of locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules is largely unambiguous in
the case of a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme
X
.

Theorem 4.27
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
and
X=\bigcup_{\alpha}U_{\alpha}
be a finite affine
open covering of
X
subordinate to
\mathbf{W}
.
Let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
and
\mathfrak{M}
be
a
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module on
X
.
In this context:

(a)
there exists a short exact sequence of
\mathbf{W}
-locally
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
0\longrightarrow\mathfrak{J}^{\prime}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{P}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{M}\longrightarrow 0

on
X
such that
\mathfrak{P}
is an antilocal contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module and

\mathfrak{J}^{\prime}
is an
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-module;

(b)
there exists a short exact sequence of
\mathbf{W}
-locally
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
0\longrightarrow\mathfrak{M}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{J}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{P}^{\prime}\longrightarrow 0

on
X
such that
\mathfrak{J}
is an
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective
\mathbf{W}
-locally
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module and
\mathfrak{P}^{\prime}
is an antilocal contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-module;

(c)
a (
\mathbf{W}
-locally) contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module on
X
is
antilocal if and only if it is a direct summand of a finitely
iterated extension of the direct images of contraherent

\mathcal{A}|_{U_{\alpha}}
-modules from
U_{\alpha}
in the exact category

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
or
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}
.

Proof.

Part (b) follows immediately from
Lemma
4.24
(because the class of
antilocal contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules is closed under extensions in

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
by
[
46
, Corollary 4.3.3(b)]
and preserved by
the direct images by
[
46
, Section 4.3 or Corollary 4.6.3(a)]
).
Part (a) follows from part (b) by virtue of the Salce lemma
[
68
]
,

[
46
, Lemma B.1.1(a)]
(cf.
[
46
, proof of Lemma 4.3.4]
).

To prove part (c), one needs to observe that the proof of part (a)
actually provides a more precise result: in part (a), one can choose
the short exact sequence so that
\mathfrak{P}
is a finitely iterated extension
of the direct images of contraherent
\mathcal{A}|_{U_{\alpha}}
-modules
from
U_{\alpha}
.
Now let
\mathfrak{Q}
be an antilocal contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module on
X
.
Choose a short exact sequence
0\longrightarrow\mathfrak{J}^{\prime}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{P}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{Q}\longrightarrow 0
,
where
\mathfrak{P}
is a finitely iterated extension of the direct images from

U_{\alpha}
as above, while
\mathfrak{J}^{\prime}
is an
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective

\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module.
Then
\Ext^{\mathcal{A},1}(\mathfrak{Q},\mathfrak{J}^{\prime})=0
by
Corollary
4.23
, hence
\mathfrak{Q}
is
a direct summand of
\mathfrak{P}
, as desired.
∎

Corollary 4.28
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
and
X=\bigcup_{\alpha}U_{\alpha}
be a finite affine
open covering of
X
subordinate to
\mathbf{W}
.
Let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
Then a
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module
\mathfrak{J}
is

\mathcal{A}
-locally injective if and only if
\Ext^{\mathcal{A},1}(\mathfrak{P},\mathfrak{J})=0

for all antilocal contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
\mathfrak{P}
.

Proof.

The “only if” assertion is provided by
Corollary
4.23
.
The “if” implication follows from the same corollary and
Theorem
4.27
(b)
by the argument dual to the proof of
Theorem
4.27
(c);
see
[
46
, Lemma B.1.2]
.
The point is that the class of
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective

\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules is closed under direct
summands in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.
∎

Remark 4.29
.

The assertions of
Theorem
4.27
(a–b)
and Corollaries
4.23

and
4.28
can be
summarized by saying that the pair of classes (antilocal contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules,
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules) is a hereditary complete cotorsion pair in the exact
category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.
On the other hand,
Theorem
4.27
(c)
is an extra piece of information which one obtains from our proof of
Theorem
4.27
(a–b).

The assertion of
Theorem
4.27
(c)
can be expressed by saying that the class of all antilocal contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules on a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme
X
is
(just as the terminology suggests)
antilocal
in the sense
of
[
54
, Section 4]
.
Together with the claim that every
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-module on
X
is an admissible subobject of an
\mathcal{A}
-locally
injective one (a weak version of
Lemma
4.24
or
Theorem
4.27
(b)),
this is the main result of this
Section
4.5
for our purposes
in this paper.

4.6.
Antilocality of antilocal
X
-locally cotorsion
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme and
\mathcal{A}
be
a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
By an “antilocal
X
-locally cotorsion contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module”
we mean a (
\mathbf{W}
-locally) contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module that is
simultaneously antilocal and
X
-locally cotorsion.

Lemma 4.30
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
and
X=\bigcup_{\alpha}U_{\alpha}
be a finite affine
open covering of
X
subordinate to
\mathbf{W}
.
Let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
and
\mathfrak{M}
be
an
X
-locally cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module
on
X
.
Then there exists a short exact sequence of
X
-locally cotorsion

\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
0\longrightarrow\mathfrak{M}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{J}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{P}\longrightarrow 0
on
X
such that
\mathfrak{J}
is an
\mathcal{A}
-locally
injective
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module and
\mathfrak{P}
is
a finitely iterated extension of the direct imags of

U_{\alpha}
-locally cotorsion contraherent
\mathcal{A}|_{U_{\alpha}}
-modules
in the exact category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
(or
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
).

Proof.

Similar to the proof of Lemma
4.24
.
Notice that the class of
X
-locally cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules is preserved by the direct images with
respect to
(\mathbf{W},\mathbf{T})
-affine open immersions of schemes
(see Section
2.4
and
the end of Section
3.6
).
∎

Lemma 4.31
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
and
X=\bigcup_{\alpha}U_{\alpha}
be a finite affine
open covering of
X
subordinate to
\mathbf{W}
.
Denote by
j_{\alpha}\colon U_{\alpha}\longrightarrow X
the open immersion morphisms.
Let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
and
\mathfrak{M}
be
an
X
-locally cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module
on
X
.
Then there exists an admissible epimorphism onto
\mathfrak{M}
in

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
from a finite direct sum
\bigoplus_{\alpha}j_{\alpha}{}_{!}\mathfrak{M}_{\alpha}
for some
U_{\alpha}
-locally cotorsion
contraherent
\mathcal{A}|_{U_{\alpha}}
-modules
\mathfrak{M}_{\alpha}
.

Proof.

This is similar to Lemma
4.25
.
∎

Theorem 4.32
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
and
X=\bigcup_{\alpha}U_{\alpha}
be a finite affine
open covering of
X
subordinate to
\mathbf{W}
.
Let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
and
\mathfrak{M}
be
an
X
-locally cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module
on
X
.
In this context:

(a)
there exists a short exact sequence of
X
-locally
cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
0\longrightarrow\mathfrak{J}^{\prime}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{P}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{M}\longrightarrow 0
on
X
such that
\mathfrak{P}
is an antilocal

X
-locally cotorsion contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module and
\mathfrak{J}^{\prime}
is
an
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module;

(b)
there exists a short exact sequence of
X
-locally
cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
0\longrightarrow\mathfrak{M}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{J}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{P}^{\prime}\longrightarrow 0
on
X
such that
\mathfrak{J}
is
an
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module
and
\mathfrak{P}^{\prime}
is an antilocal
X
-locally cotorsion contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-module;

(c)
an
X
-locally cotorsion (
\mathbf{W}

\mathcal{A}

of a finitely iterated extension of the direct images of

-modules
in the exact category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
or
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
.

Proof.

Similar to
Theorem
4.27
, and
based on Corollary
4.23

and Lemmas
4.30
–
4.31
.
∎

Corollary 4.33
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
and
X=\bigcup_{\alpha}U_{\alpha}
be a finite affine
open covering of
X
subordinate to
\mathbf{W}
.
Let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
Then an
X
-locally cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-module
\mathfrak{J}
is
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective if and only if

\Ext^{\mathcal{A},1}(\mathfrak{P},\mathfrak{J})=0
for all antilocal
X
-locally cotorsion
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
\mathfrak{P}
.

Proof.

Similar to
Corollary
4.28
.
∎

Remark 4.34
.

The assertions of
Theorem
4.32
(a–b)
together with
Corollaries
4.33

and
4.23

(with Remark
4.26
) can be summarized by saying
that the pair of classes (antilocal
X
-locally cotorsion contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules,
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules) is a hereditary complete cotorsion pair in the exact
category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
.
This assertion does not need a separate proof: it can be deduced from
the similar result for the exact category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
as per
Remark
4.29
by applying
the lemma about restricting (hereditary) complete cotorsion pairs to
exact subcategories
[
54
, Lemmas 1.5(b) and 1.6]
,

[
46
, Lemmas B.1.3 and B.1.4(b)]
.

On the other hand,
Theorem
4.32
(c)
is an extra piece of information which one obtains from the proof of
Theorem
4.32
(a–b)
sketched above.
Theorem
4.32
(c)
does
not
follow from
Theorem
4.27
(c) (or
any other results of Sections
4.4
–
4.5
) in any simple way;
instead, its proof requires going through all the rounds of
the argument again in the new context, as described in the present
Section
4.6
.

The assertion of
Theorem
4.32
(c)
can be expressed by saying that the class of all antilocal

X
-locally cotorsion contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules on a quasi-compact
semi-separated scheme
X
is
antilocal
in the sense
of
[
54
, Section 4]
.
This is the main result of this
Section
4.6
for our purposes
in this paper.

4.7.
Antilocality of antilocal
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
and
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
By an “antilocal
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module”
we mean a (
\mathbf{W}
-locally) contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module that is
simultaneously antilocal and
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion.

Remark 4.35
.

Everything that was said in the previous
Section
4.6
remains valid
with the
X
-locally cotorsion condition on
\mathbf{W}
-locally
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules replaced by the
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion
condition.

It suffices to replace the words “
X
-locally cotorsion” by
the words “
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion”, the words
“
U_{\alpha}
-locally cotorsion” by the words
“
\mathcal{A}|_{U_{\alpha}}
-locally cotorsion”, and the upper index
“
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}
” with the upper index “
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}
” in the notation for
the exact categories throughout
the whole Section
4.6

in order to obtain a correct exposition of the theory of antilocality
of antilocal
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
and the related cotorsion pair.
One can also point out that the class of
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion

\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules is preserved by
the direct images with respect to
(\mathbf{W},\mathbf{T})
-affine open immersions
of schemes, as explained in
Section
3.5
.
To avoid unnecessary repetition, we mostly restrict ourselves to this
short remark in the present section.

The following corollary, which is an
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion version
of
[
46
, Corollary 4.6.3(a–b)]
, illustrates the power of
the main result obtained in this way (viz., the
\mathcal{A}
-locally
cotorsion version of
Theorem
4.32
(c)).

Let us introduce some notation first.
The full exact subcategory of antilocal contraherent cosheaves on
a scheme
X
(defined in
[
46
, Section 4.3]
, see
Section
4.4
) will be denoted by

X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}\subset X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}
.

We will denote the full subcategory of antilocal contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules (defined in
Sections
4.4
–
4.5
) by

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.
The full subcategory of antilocal
X
-locally cotorsion contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules (defined in
Section
4.6
) will be denoted
by
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
.
The full subcategory of antilocal
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules (defined above in this section) will be denoted
by
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
.

Corollary 4.36
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme and
Y
be
a quasi-compact open subscheme in
X
with the open immersion morphism

f\colon Y\longrightarrow X
.
Let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
Then the direct image functor
f_{!}\colon(Y,\mathcal{A}|_{Y}){\operatorname{\mathsf{--Cosh}}}\longrightarrow(X,\mathcal{A}){\operatorname{\mathsf{--Cosh}}}
takes antilocal
\mathcal{A}|_{Y}
-locally cotorsion contraherent
cosheaves on
Y
to antilocal
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion contraherent
cosheaves on
X
.
The resulting functor of exact categories

f_{!}\colon\mathcal{A}|_{Y}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{\mathcal{A}|_{Y}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}_{\mathsf{al}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}_{\mathsf{al}}

is exact.

Proof.

Notice first of all that the direct image functor

f_{!}\colon(Y,\mathcal{O}_{Y}){\operatorname{\mathsf{--Cosh}}}\longrightarrow(X,\mathcal{O}_{X}){\operatorname{\mathsf{--Cosh}}}
takes antilocal contraherent
cosheaves on
Y
to antilocal contraherent cosheaves on
X
, and
the resulting functor
f_{!}\colon Y{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}\longrightarrow X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}
is exact
(for any morphism of quasi-compact semi-separated schemes

f\colon Y\longrightarrow X
) by
[
46
, Corollary 4.6.3(a)]
, which was
already mentioned in Section
4.4
.
On the other hand, for an
affine
open immersion morphism

f\colon Y\longrightarrow X
, the functor
f_{!}\colon(Y,\mathcal{A}|_{Y}){\operatorname{\mathsf{--Cosh}}}\longrightarrow(X,\mathcal{A}){\operatorname{\mathsf{--Cosh}}}
takes all (and not only antilocal)
\mathcal{A}|_{Y}
-locally
cotorsion contraherent cosheaves on
Y
to
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion
contrarent cosheaves on
X

by Lemma
1.6
(a),
as mentioned in Section
3.5
.
This suffices to prove the corollary in the case when the open
immersion morphism
f
is affine.

In the full generality of the assumptions of the corollary, it follows
from
[
46
, Corollary 4.6.3(a)]
that the functor

f_{!}\colon(Y,\mathcal{A}|_{Y}){\operatorname{\mathsf{--Cosh}}}\longrightarrow(X,\mathcal{A}){\operatorname{\mathsf{--Cosh}}}
takes antilocal contraherent

\mathcal{A}|_{Y}
-modules on
Y
to antilocal contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules on

X
, and the resulting functor
f_{!}\colon\mathcal{A}|_{Y}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}

is exact.
By Theorem
4.32
(c)
and Remark
4.35
, all
the antilocal
\mathcal{A}|_{Y}
-locally cotorsion contraherent
\mathcal{A}|_{Y}
-modules
on
Y
are direct summands of finitely iterated extensions of the direct
images of
\mathcal{A}|_{Y}
-locally cotorsion contraherent
\mathcal{A}|_{Y}
-modules
from affine open subschemes
V\subset Y
.
One can consider such finitely iterated extensions as happening in
the exact category of antilocal
\mathcal{A}|_{Y}
-modules
\mathcal{A}|_{Y}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}
.
The functor
f_{!}\colon\mathcal{A}|_{Y}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}
preserves
finitely iterated extensions, since it is exact.
The assertions of the corollary follow.
∎

5.
Naïve Co-Contra Correspondence for
\mathcal{A}
-Modules

The aim of this section is to construct a triangulated equivalence

\mathsf{D}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}})\simeq\mathsf{D}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}})
between the derived category
of the abelian category of quasi-coherent sheaves of
\mathcal{A}
-modules and
the derived category of the exact category of
\mathbf{W}
-locally
contraherent cosheaves of
\mathcal{A}
-modules for a quasi-compact
semi-separated scheme
X
with an open covering
\mathbf{W}
and
a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra
\mathcal{A}
over
X
.

5.1.
Resolution and coresolution dimensions

Let
\mathsf{E}
be an exact category (in the sense of Quillen
[
12
]
).
A full subcategory
\mathsf{F}\subset\mathsf{E}
is said to be
generating

if every object of
\mathsf{E}
is an admissible quotient object of
an object from
\mathsf{F}
.
A generating full subcategory
\mathsf{F}\subset\mathsf{E}
is said to be

resolving
if
\mathsf{F}
is closed under extensions and kernels of
admissible epimorphisms in
\mathsf{E}
.

Dually, a full subcategory
\mathsf{C}\subset\mathsf{E}
is said to be

cogenerating
if every object of
\mathsf{E}
is an admissible
suboboject of and object from
\mathsf{F}
.
A cogenerating full subcategory
\mathsf{C}\subset\mathsf{E}
is said to be

coresolving
if
\mathsf{C}
is closed under extensions and cokernels
of admissible subobjects in
\mathsf{E}
.

Any (co)resolving full subcategory in
\mathsf{E}
is, by definition,
closed under extensions; so it inherits an exact category structure.
We will consider the (co)resolving full subcategories in
\mathsf{E}
as
exact categories with the inherited exact category structure.

Given an additive category
\mathsf{A}
, we denote by
\mathsf{K}^{\star}(\mathsf{A})

the category of complexes in
\mathsf{A}
with morphisms up to the cochain
homotopy.
We refer to
[
12
, Section 10.4]
for the definition of
the derived category
\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathsf{E})
of an exact category
\mathsf{E}
.
Here
\star
is one of the conventional derived category symbols

\star=\mathsf{b}
,
+
,
-
, or
\varnothing
.

Proposition 5.1
.

Let
\mathsf{F}
be a resolving subcategory in an exact category
\mathsf{E}
.
Then the inclusion of the exact categories
\mathsf{F}\longrightarrow\mathsf{E}
induces
an equivalence of the derived categories

\mathsf{D}^{-}(\mathsf{F})\simeq\mathsf{D}^{-}(\mathsf{E}).

Proof.

This is the dual version of
[
36
, Theorem 12.1]
,
or the dual version of
[
35
, Proposition 13.2.2(i)]
,
or
[
46
, Proposition A.3.1(a)]
.
∎

The classical homological algebra concepts of projective, injective,
and flat dimensions can be extended to exact categories with
(co)resolving subcategories as follows.
Assume that the exact category
\mathsf{E}
is weakly
idempotent-complete
[
12
, Section 7]
; then so is any
(co)resolving subcategory in
\mathsf{E}
.

Let
\mathsf{F}\subset\mathsf{E}
be a resolving subcategory and
d\geq-1
be
an integer.
An object
E\in\mathsf{E}
is said to have
resolution dimension
\leq d

with respect to
\mathsf{F}
if there exists an exact sequence

0\longrightarrow F_{d}\longrightarrow F_{d-1}\longrightarrow\dotsb\longrightarrow F_{0}\longrightarrow E\longrightarrow 0

in
\mathsf{E}
.

Dually, let
\mathsf{C}\subset\mathsf{E}
be a coresolving subcategory.
An object
E\in\mathsf{E}
is said to have
coresolution
dimension
\leq d
with respect to
\mathsf{C}
if there exists an exact
sequence
0\longrightarrow E\longrightarrow C^{0}\longrightarrow C^{1}\longrightarrow\dotsb\longrightarrow C^{d}\longrightarrow 0
in
\mathsf{E}
.

Just as the projective, injective, and flat dimensions,
the (co)resolution dimension does not depend on the choice of
a (co)resolution.
A precise formulation and proof this result can be found,
on various generality levels, in
[
73
, Lemma 2.1]
,

[
70
, Proposition 2.3(1)]
, or
[
46
, Corollary A.5.2]
.

Proposition 5.2
.

Let
\mathsf{F}
be a resolving subcategory in a weakly idempotent-complete
exact category
\mathsf{E}
such that the resolution dimensions of all
the objects of
\mathsf{E}
with respect to
\mathsf{F}
are finite and bounded
by a fixed constant
d
.
Then, for any conventional derived category symbol
\star=\mathsf{b}
,
+
,

-
, or
\varnothing
, the inclusion of exact categories

\mathsf{F}\longrightarrow\mathsf{E}
induces an equivalence of the derived categories

\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathsf{F})\simeq\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathsf{E}).

Proof.

The case
\star=-
is covered by the more general
Proposition
5.1
.
The case
\star=\mathsf{b}
is the dual version
of
[
35
, Proposition 13.2.2(ii)]
.
The case
\star=\varnothing
is
[
70
, Proposition 5.14]
.
The result of
[
46
, Proposition A.5.8]
covers all
the cases.
∎

5.2.
Cotorsion periodicity

Periodicity theorems
in homological algebra are linked to
properties of the objects of cocycles in acyclic complexes.
These results and their corollaries describe the phenomena when certain
(co)resolving subcategories exhibit behavior typical of such
subcategories with finite (co)resolution dimensions, even though
the (co)resolution dimensions are actually infinite.
We refer to the papers
[
4
,
61
]
and
the preprint
[
46
, Section 7]
for a general discussion of
the periodicity theorems from the perspective relevant to
the present paper.

The following
cotorsion periodicity theorem
for modules over
associative rings is due to Bazzoni, Cortés-Izurdiaga, and
Estrada
[
3
, Theorem 1.2(2), Proposition 4.8(2), or
Theorem 5.1(2)]
.
The definition of a cotorsion
R
-module can be found in
Section
1.2
.

Theorem 5.3
.

Let
R
be an associative ring and
C^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
be an acyclic complex
of
R
-modules.
Assume that all the terms
C^{n}
,
n\in\mathbb{Z}
, of the complex
C^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}

are cotorsion
R
-modules.
Then all the
R
-modules of cocycles of the acyclic complex
C^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}

are also cotorsion
R
-modules. ∎

The following theorem is a quasi-coherent sheaf version of
the preceding one.
The definition of a cotorsion quasi-coherent sheaf on a scheme can be
found in Section
4.2
.

Theorem 5.4
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme and
\mathcal{C}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
be
an acyclic complex of quasi-coherent sheaves on
X
.
Assume that all the terms
\mathcal{C}^{n}
,
n\in\mathbb{Z}
, of the complex
\mathcal{C}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}

are cotorsion quasi-coherent sheaves.
Then all the quasi-coherent sheaves of cocycles of the acyclic complex

\mathcal{C}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
are also cotorsion.

Proof.

This is
[
61
, Corollary 10.4]
.
A version of this approach was developed by Estrada with collaborators;
see
[
61
, Remark 10.3]
for a discussion.
Another proof can be found in
[
55
, Section 8]
.
∎

The next theorem is a common generalization of
Theorems
5.3

and
5.4
.
The definition of an
\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-module
can be found in Section
4.3
.

Theorem 5.5
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme and
\mathcal{A}
be
a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
Let
\mathcal{C}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
be an acyclic complex of quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
on
X
.
Assume that all the terms
\mathcal{C}^{n}
,
n\in\mathbb{Z}
, of the complex
\mathcal{C}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}

are
\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules.
Then all the quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules of cocycles of the acyclic
complex
\mathcal{C}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
are also
\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion.

Proof.

The argument from
[
61
, proof of Corollary 10.4]
, based as it is
on numerous preceding results of
[
61
]
and the category-theoretic
references therein, works in the situation at hand without any changes.
The result of
Lemma
1.23
(a), establishing
the local nature of the class of flat quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules,
is helpful.
One proves that all flat quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules are filtered
inductive limits of locally countably presented flat quasi-coherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules, shows that the latter ones have finite projective
dimensions in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
, etc.
We omit the straightforward but tedious details.
The contraherent cosheaf-based argument from
[
55
, Section 8]

can be also adopted to the situation at hand, using the next
Corollary
5.6
,
the constructions of
Sections
5.5

and
5.7
,
and Lemma
5.23
below.
∎

The locally contraherent version of the preceding results follows
from the module-theoretic version.
The definitions of
X
-locally cotorsion and
\mathcal{A}
-locally
cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules can be found
in Sections
3.3

and
3.4
.

Corollary 5.6
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
, and let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra
over
X
.
Let
\mathfrak{C}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
be an acyclic complex in the exact category

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.
In this context:

(a)
If all the terms
\mathfrak{C}^{n}
,
n\in\mathbb{Z}
, of the complex

\mathfrak{C}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
are
X
-locally cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules, then the complex
\mathfrak{C}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
is acyclic in the exact
category of
X
-locally cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
.

(b)
If all the terms
\mathfrak{C}^{n}
,
n\in\mathbb{Z}
, of the complex

\mathfrak{C}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
are
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules, then the complex
\mathfrak{C}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
is acyclic in the exact
category of
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
.

Proof.

Both the assertions follow immediately from
Theorem
5.3
(applied to the rings

R=\mathcal{O}_{X}(U)
in the case (a) and to the rings
R=\mathcal{A}(U)
in
the case (b), where
U
ranges over the affine open subschemes

U\subset X
subordinate to
\mathbf{W}
).
∎

5.3.
Background derived equivalences for quasi-coherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules

In this section we prove
\mathcal{A}
-module generalizations of some
results about the conventional derived categories of quasi-coherent
sheaves from
[
46
, Section 4.7]
.

Let
X
be a scheme and
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra
over
X
.
We will denote the full subcategory of
X
-contraadjusted quasi-coherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules (defined in
Section
4.1
)
by
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cta}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
.
The full subcategory of
X
-cotorsion quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
(defined in Section
4.2
) will be
denoted by
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
.
The full subcategory of
\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
(defined in Section
4.3
) will be
denoted by
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
.

Lemma 5.7
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an affine
open covering
X=\bigcup_{\alpha=1}^{N}U_{\alpha}
and
\mathcal{A}
be
a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
Then

(a)
the full subcategories of
X
-contraadjusted,

X
-cotorsion, and
\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion quasi-coherent sheaves

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cta}}}}
,
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}}
, and
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}}

are coresolving in the abelian category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
;

(b)
all quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules have finite coresolution
dimensions not exceeding
N
with respect to the coresolving
subcategory
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cta}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
.

Proof.

Part (a): recall that one has
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cta}}}}
, as explained in
Section
4.3
.
Clearly, all injective quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules are

\mathcal{A}
-cotorsion.
So all the three full subcategories in question are cogenerating
in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
.
All the three full subcategories are also closed under extensions
(by the definition) and under cokernels of monomorphisms
(by
[
46
, Corollary 4.1.2(c)]
,
[
46
, Corollary 4.1.9(c)]
,
and Corollary
4.16
(c) above,
respectively).

Part (b): the assertion follows from the facts that the coresolution
dimension does not depend on the choice of a coresolution
(see Section
5.1
) and that all
quasi-coherent sheaves on
X
have finite coresolution dimensions
not exceeding
N
with respect to the coresolving subcategory
of contraadjusted quasi-coherent sheaves
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{\mathsf{cta}}\subset X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}

[
46
, Lemma 4.7.1(b)]
.
∎

Corollary 5.8
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme and
\mathcal{A}
be
a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
Then, for any conventional derived category symbol
\star=\mathsf{b}
,
+
,

-
, or
\varnothing
, the inclusion of exact/abelian categories

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cta}}}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
induces an equivalence of
the derived categories

\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cta}}}})\simeq\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}).

Proof.

Follows from
Lemma
5.7
(a–b)
in view of the dual version of Proposition
5.2
.
∎

Theorem 5.9
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme and
\mathcal{A}
be
a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
Then, for any derived category symbol
\star=+
or
\varnothing
,
the inclusions of exact/abelian categories

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}}\longrightarrow X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
induce
equivalences of the derived categories

\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}})\simeq\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}})\simeq\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}).

Proof.

It suffices to show that the triangulated functors

\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}})\longrightarrow\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}})
and

\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}})\longrightarrow\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}})
are
triangulated equivalences.
The case
\star=+
is easy and follows from
Lemma
5.7
(a)
by virtue of the dual version of Proposition
5.1
.
The case of the doubly unbounded derived categories
(
\star=\varnothing
) is interesting and based on the cotorsion
periodicity (Theorems
5.4

and
5.5
).

It is convenient to refer to
[
61
, Proposition 10.6]
.
In view of this proposition, in order to prove that the functor

\mathsf{D}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}})\longrightarrow\mathsf{D}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}})
is a triangulated
equivalence, it suffices to check that a complex in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}}

is acyclic in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}}
whenever it is acyclic in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
.
This assertion follows from the fact that a complex in the exact
category of cotorsion quasi-coherent sheaves
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{\mathsf{cot}}
is
acyclic in
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{\mathsf{cot}}
whenever it is acyclic in
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
.
This is Theorem
5.4
.

By the same proposition from
[
61
]
, in order to prove that

\mathsf{D}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}})\longrightarrow\mathsf{D}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}})
is a triangulated
equivalence, it suffices to check that a complex in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}}

is acyclic in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-cot}}}}
whenever it is acyclic
in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Qcoh}}}
.
This is Theorem
5.5
.
∎

5.4.
Background derived equivalences for locally
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules

In this section we prove
\mathcal{A}
-module generalizations of some
results about the conventional derived categories of locally
contraherent cosheaves from
[
46
, Section 4.7]
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
and
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
We refer to Section
4.7

for the notation
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}_{\mathsf{al}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}_{\mathsf{al}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}
for the exact categories of antilocal
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules.
The similar notation
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}
for the exact category of
antilocal contraherent cosheaves on
X
was also mentioned there.

Lemma 5.10
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
and a finite affine open covering

X=\bigcup_{\alpha=1}^{N}U_{\alpha}
subordinate to
\mathbf{W}
.
Let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra over
X
.
In this context:

(a)
The full subcategories
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
of contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules and antilocal
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules are resolving in the exact category
of
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.
All the objects of
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
have finite resolution dimensions
not exceeding
N-1
with respect to the resolving subcategories

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}
and
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}
.

(b)
The full subcategories
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
of
X
-locally cotorsion
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules and antilocal
X
-locally cotorsion
contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules are resolving in the exact category
of
X
-locally cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
.
All the objects of
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
have finite resolution
dimensions not exceeding
N-1
with respect to the resolving
subcategories
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
and
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
.

(c)
The full subcategories
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
of
\mathcal{A}
-locally
cotorsion contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules and antilocal
\mathcal{A}
-locally
cotorsion contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules are resolving in the exact
category of
\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent

\mathcal{A}
-modules
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
.
All the objects of
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
have finite resolution
dimensions not exceeding
N-1
with respect to the resolving
subcategories
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
and
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
.

Proof.

The full subcategory
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}
is closed under extensions and
kernels of admissible epimorphisms in the exact category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}

in view of the discussion in
Section
2.3
.
It follows that the full subcategory
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
is closed
under extensions and kernels of admissible epimorphisms in
the exact category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
, and the full subcategory

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
is closed under extensions and kernels of
admissible epimorphisms in the exact category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
.

The full subcategory
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}
is closed under extensions and
kernels of admissible epimorphisms in the exact category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
,
since the full subcategory of antilocal contraherent cosheaves

X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}
is closed under extensions and kernels of admissible
epimorphisms in the exact category
X{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.
See
[
46
, Corollary 4.3.3(b)]
and the discussion in
Section
4.4
.
The full subcategory
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
is closed under extensions
and kernels of admissible epimorphisms in the exact category

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
, and the full subcategory

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
is closed under extensions and kernels of
admissible epimorphisms in the exact category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
,
for the same reasons.

Every object of
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
is an admissible quotient of an object
from
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}
, every object of
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
is
an admissible quotient of an object from
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
, and
every object of
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
is an admissible quotient of
an object from
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
by
Lemmas
4.25

and
4.31
,
and Remark
4.35
.
Finally, the resolution dimensions do not exceed
N-1
because
the Čech resolution (
23
)
is a resolution by antilocal contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules.
(Cf.
[
46
, Lemmas 4.7.1(b) and 4.7.2(a–b)]
.)
∎

Corollary 5.11
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
, and let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra
over
X
.
Let
\star=\mathsf{b}
,
+
,
-
, or
\varnothing
be a conventional
derived category symbol.
Then

(a)
the inclusions of exact categories
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
induce equivalences of
the derived categories

\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}})\simeq\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}})\simeq\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}});

(b)
the inclusions of exact categories
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
induce
equivalences of the derived categories

\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}})\simeq\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}})\simeq\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}});

(c)
the inclusions of exact categories

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
induce equivalences of the derived categories

\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}_{\mathsf{al}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}})\simeq\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}})\simeq\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}).

Proof.

All the assertions follow from the respective assertions of
Lemma
5.10
in view of
Proposition
5.2
.
∎

Lemma 5.12
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
, and let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra
over
X
.
Then the full subcategories of
X
-locally cotorsion and

\mathcal{A}
-locally cotorsion
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
and
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
are coresolving
in the exact category of
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.

Proof.

The full subcategories in question are cogenerating in

\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
by Lemma
4.24

(recall that
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
by
Lemma
1.6
(a), as pointed
out in Section
3.4
).
The two full subcategories are also closed under extensions and
cokernels of admissible monomorphisms in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
by
Lemma
1.2
(b), as mentioned in
Sections
2.3

and
3.4
.
∎

Lemma 5.13
.

Let
R
be an associative ring and
C^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
be a complex of left

R
-modules.
Then there exists a complex of injective left
R
-modules
J^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}

together with a termwise injective morphism of complexes of
R
-modules

C^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}\longrightarrow J^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
such that the quotient complex
J^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}/C^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}

is acyclic.

Proof.

In fact, the requirements in the lemma are weak and can be strengthened
in various ways: in particular, one can require the complex
J^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
to
be a
homotopy injective
complex of injective modules, or
alternatively, one can require the complex
J^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}/C^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
to be

coacyclic in the sense of Becker
(but not both simultaneously).
One can start by noticing an easy construction of a termwise injective
morphism of any complex
C^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
into a contractible complex of injective
modules
K^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
.
Then one can find a morphism from
C^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
into a complex of injective
modules
I^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
such that the cone of the morphism
C^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}\longrightarrow I^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}

is acyclic, as per
[
61
, proof of Proposition 10.6]
.
Finally, put
J^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}=I^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}\oplus K^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
, and consider the diagonal
morphism
C^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}\longrightarrow I^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}\oplus K^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
.
The same arguments work in any Grothendieck category in place of

R{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Mod}}}
.
∎

Lemma 5.14
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
, and let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra
over
X
.
Then, for any complex of
\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules

\mathfrak{M}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
on
X
, there exists a complex of
\mathcal{A}
-locally injective

\mathbf{W}
-locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-modules
\mathfrak{J}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
on
X
together
with a morphism of complexes
\mathfrak{M}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{J}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
that is
a termwise admissible monomorphism in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
such that
the cokernel
\mathfrak{J}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}/\mathfrak{M}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
is acyclic in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
.

Proof.

This is a (weak) locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module version
of
[
46
, Lemmas 4.7.8 and 4.9.4]
.
The idea is to plug in whole complexes of cosheaves
(rather than single cosheaves) into the construction of
Lemmas
4.24

and
4.30
.
One has to use locality of the notion of an
\mathcal{A}
-locally
injective locally contraherent
\mathcal{A}
-module, together with the facts
that the class of such modules is closed under extensions and
cokernels of monomorphisms in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
and preserved by
the direct images with respect to
(\mathbf{W},\mathbf{T})
-affine open
immersions of schemes.
One also needs to use
Lemma
5.13
.
The construction produces a (termwise admissible) short exact sequence

0\longrightarrow\mathfrak{M}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{J}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}\longrightarrow\mathfrak{P}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}\longrightarrow 0
of complexes
in the exact category
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
, where

\mathfrak{J}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}\in\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}
and
\mathfrak{P}^{\text{\smaller\smaller$\scriptstyle\bullet$}}
is a finitely iterated
extension of the direct images of acyclic complexes in the exact
categories
\mathcal{A}|_{U_{\alpha}}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Ctrh}}}
of contraherent

\mathcal{A}|_{U_{\alpha}}
-modules on
U_{\alpha}
.
Here
X=\bigcup_{\alpha}U_{\alpha}
is some finite affine open covering
of
X
subordinate to
\mathbf{W}
.
∎

Theorem 5.15
.

Let
X
be a quasi-compact semi-separated scheme with an open
covering
\mathbf{W}
, and let
\mathcal{A}
be a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra
over
X
.
Then, for any derived category symbol
\star=+
or
\varnothing
,
the inclusions of exact categories
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\longrightarrow\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
induce equivalences
of the derived categories

\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}})\simeq\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}})\simeq\mathsf{D}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}).

Proof.

The easy case
\star=+
follows from Lemma
5.12

by virtue of the dual version of Proposition
5.1
.
The interesting case
\star=\varnothing
follows from
Corollary
5.6

and Lemma
5.14
by virtue of
a well-known lemma about localizations of categories by multiplicative
subsets
[
35
, Proposition 10.2.7(i)]
,
[
45
, Lemma 1.6(b)]
,
or
[
46
, Lemma A.3.3(b)]
.
The point is that Lemma
5.14

provides a quasi-isomorphism in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
from any given complex
in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}
to a complex in
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}
, and one
has
\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lin}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}\subset\mathcal{A}{\operatorname{\mathsf{--Lcth}}}_{\mathbf{W}}^{X{\operatorname{\mathsf{-lct}}}}
.
∎

5.5.
Contraherent
\fHom
over a quasi-coherent quasi-algebra

This section is a partial extension of
[
46
, Section 2.5]

from
\mathcal{O}_{X}
-modules to
\mathcal{A}
-modules.
Let
X
be a scheme, and let
\mathcal{A}
and
\mathcal{B}
be two quasi-coherent
quasi-algebras over
X
.

A
quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-
\mathcal{B}
-bimodule

\mathcal{E}
on
X
is
a quasi-coherent quasi-module on
X
endowed with a structure of
a sheaf of
\mathcal{A}
-
\mathcal{B}
-bimodules satisfying the following condition.
The
\mathcal{O}_{X}
-module structure underlying the left
\mathcal{A}
-module
structure on
\mathcal{E}
must coincide with the left
\mathcal{O}_{X}
-module structure
on the quasi-coherent quasi-module
\mathcal{E}
, and the
\mathcal{O}_{X}
-module
structure underlying the right
\mathcal{B}
-module structure on
\mathcal{E}
must
coincide with the right
\mathcal{O}_{X}
-module structure on the quasi-coherent
quasi-module
\mathcal{E}
on
X
.
Equivalently, a structure of quasi-coherent
\mathcal{A}
-
\mathcal{B}
-bimodule
on a quasi-coherent quasi-module
\mathcal{E}
on
X
is given by
an
action map

\mathcal{A}\otimes_{\mathcal{O}_{X}}\mathcal{E}\otimes_{\mathcal{O}_{X}}\mathcal{B}\longrightarrow\mathcal{E}
,
which must be a morphism of quasi-coherent quasi-modules on
X

(cf. Lemma
3.4
(a))
satisfying the usual associativity and unitality axioms.

Let
\mathbf{B}
denote the topology base of
X
consisting of all
the affine open subschemes
U\subset X
.
Let
\mathcal{F}
be a quasi-coherent left
\mathcal{A}
-module on
X
and
R
be
an associative ring.
Assume that
R
acts on
\mathcal{F}
on the right by left
\mathcal{A}
-module
endomorphisms.
Given a left
R
-module
M
, define a presheaf of
\mathcal{A}
-modules

\mathcal{F}\otimes_{R}M
on
\mathbf{B}
by the rule

(\mathcal{F}\otimes_{R}M)(U)=\mathcal{F}(U)\otimes_{R}M

for all
U\in\mathbf{B}
.
One can easily check that (the underlying presheaf of
\mathcal{O}_{X}
-modules
of)
\mathcal{F}\otimes_{R}M
satisfies the quasi-coherence axiom from
Section
2.2
.
By Lemma
2.2

and Theorem
2.1
